# Commands · S · part 31

1,506 entries, sw-execute to sync. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **sw-execute**: Execute one phase-sized slice on the current branch using the spec union and per-task execute discipline. Does not commit, push, or open a PR. ([grdavies/shipwright](https://github.com/grdavies/shipwright/blob/HEAD/core/commands/sw-execute.md) · ★2 · 3 copies)

([grdavies/shipwright](https://github.com/grdavies/shipwright/blob/HEAD/core/commands/sw-explore.md) · ★2 · 3 copies)

- **sw-plan**: Turns a reviewed feature into an implementation plan and traceable agent tasks, split per layer (backend, frontend, mobile, devops) with a layer prefix, each task tracing to acceptance criteria, rules, optional code paths and the mockups it builds, with a coverage check that flags stale tasks. With no feature named it plans every accepted feature still without a current plan. Use when asked to plan FEAT-001, break a story into tasks, prepare work for an AI agent or developer, or check that every AC has a task. Do NOT use for unreviewed ideas (use sw-shape), writing criteria (use sw-spec) or p… ([jonaskahn/specwright](https://github.com/jonaskahn/specwright/blob/HEAD/commands/sw-plan.md) · ★1)

- **sw-review**: Runs a read-only review and records verifiable human approval, either the owner's plain accept or a formal sign-off with a hash-bound evidence link, before a draft can be planned or published. With no feature named it reviews every submitted feature together and asks once to accept the listed ones. - **sw-start**: Specwright's single entry. Detects the workspace and its state, routes to one of the public skills (sw-shape, sw-ingest, sw-spec, sw-plan, sw-review, sw-publish, sw-view), and tidies up after a step (assets, graph, trace, lint) before stopping at the next human decision. Use when starting Specwright, when unsure which sw- skill applies, or for any Specwright request that names no sw- skill. Do NOT use for writing requirements, recording approval or remote writes; those stay inside their owning skills.
